```js
 const frontendSurface=function referenceFrontendSurface() {

 const controls=[...document.querySelectorAll('a[href],button,input,[role="button"],[role="radio"],[role="checkbox"],[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')].filter(shown).map((n,i)=>{
  const r=rect(n),hit=document.elementFromPoint(r[0]+r[2]/2,r[1]+r[3]/2);
  return {id:'surface-action-'+i,tag:n.tagName,test:n.getAttribute('data-test'),name:n.getAttribute('aria-label')||n.innerText||n.getAttribute('placeholder')||'',role:n.getAttribute('role')||({BUTTON:'button',A:'link',INPUT:n.type==='radio'?'radio':'textbox'}[n.tagName]??null),rect:r,enabled:!n.disabled&&n.getAttribute('aria-disabled')!=='true',checked:typeof n.checked==='boolean'?n.checked:n.getAttribute('aria-checked'),intersects_viewport:intersects(r),center_hit:!!hit&&(hit===n||n.contains(hit)),css:css(n)};
 });
 const text_runs=[];const walker=document.createTreeWalker(document.body,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
 while(walker.nextNode()) {
  const n=walker.currentNode,p=n.parentElement,text=n.textContent.trim();if(!text||!p||!shown(p)||['SCRIPT','STYLE','NOSCRIPT'].includes(p.tagName))continue;
  const range=document.createRange();range.selectNodeContents(n);const b=range.getBoundingClientRect(),r=[b.x,b.y,b.width,b.height];if(!intersects(r))continue;
  const clipping=[];for(let a=p;a&&a!==document.body;a=a.parentElement){const s=getComputedStyle(a);if(/hidden|clip|scroll|auto/.test(s.overflow+s.overflowX+s.overflowY))clipping.push({rect:rect(a),overflow:[s.overflowX,s.overflowY]})}
  text_runs.push({text,tag:p.tagName,test:p.getAttribute('data-test'),rect:r,css:css(p),clipping});
 }
 return {path:location.pathname,viewport:[innerWidth,innerHeight],observed_at:new Date().toISOString(),actions:controls.filter(a=>a.intersects_viewport),control_inventory:controls,text_runs,limitations:['Rendered DOM ranges retain clipping ancestors; clipped text inventory is not automatically visible truth','Hit-test and viewport reachability are separate from executed interaction coverage','Audio, motion and future behavior require separate observations']};
};
 const identity=function challengeIdentity(expected, actual) {
  const normalize = value => String(value ?? '').normalize('NFKC').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const multiset = values => values.map(normalize).sort();
  const choices = expected.type === 'match'
    ? expected.pairs.flatMap(p => [p.fromToken, p.learningToken])
    : expected.type === 'listenMatch' ? expected.pairs.map(p => p.translation)
    : expected.type === 'listenIsolation' ? expected.options.map(p => p.text)
    : (expected.choices ?? []).map(c => typeof c === 'string' ? c : c.phrase ?? c.text);
  const reasons = [];
  const prompt = expected.prompt ?? (expected.type === 'listenIsolation' ? expected.tokens.map(t => t.value).join('') : null);
  if (!actual.type.split(/\s+/).includes('challenge-' + expected.type)) reasons.push('type_changed');
  const expectedBank = multiset(choices), actualBank = multiset(actual.choices);
  if (JSON.stringify(expectedBank) !== JSON.stringify(actualBank)) reasons.push('choice_set_changed');
  // The visible container is broader than a dedicated prompt node. This is
  // evidence of presence, not exact prompt equality; retain that limitation.
  const exactPrompts = actual.prompts ?? [];
  const audioPrompt = ['listenTap','listen','listenComplete'].includes(expected.type);
  if (audioPrompt) {
    if (!expected.tts || !(actual.playedAudio ?? []).includes(expected.tts)) reasons.push('audio_identity_unverified');
    if (expected.type === 'listenComplete') {
      const visibleSentence = expected.displayTokens.filter(t => !t.isBlank).map(t => t.text).join('');
      if (!normalize(actual.text).includes(normalize(visibleSentence))) reasons.push('completion_context_changed');
    }
  } else if (expected.type==='orderTapComplete') {
    const scaffold=actual.completionScaffold;
    const compact=value=>normalize(value).replace(/\s/g,'');
    const context=(expected.displayTokens??[]).filter(t=>!t.isBlank).map(t=>t.text).join('');
    let runs=0,inBlank=false;
    for(const token of expected.displayTokens??[]){if(token.isBlank){if(!inBlank)runs++;inBlank=true;}else if(token.text.trim()){inBlank=false;}}
    if(!scaffold||!context.trim()||compact(scaffold.text)!==compact(context)||scaffold.blankCount!==runs)reasons.push('completion_structure_changed');
    if(!expected.example||!exactPrompts.some(p=>normalize(p)===normalize(expected.example)))reasons.push('completion_example_changed');
  } else if (prompt && exactPrompts.length && !exactPrompts.some(p => normalize(p) === normalize(prompt))) reasons.push('prompt_changed');
  else if (prompt && !exactPrompts.length && !normalize(actual.text).includes(normalize(prompt))) reasons.push('prompt_not_visible');
  if (['tapComplete','gapFill'].includes(expected.type)) {
    const context=(expected.displayTokens??[]).filter(t=>!t.isBlank).map(t=>t.text).join('');
    if (!context.trim() || !normalize(actual.text).replace(/\s/g,'').includes(normalize(context).replace(/\s/g,''))) reasons.push('completion_context_changed');
  }
  return {matches: reasons.length === 0, reasons, expected: {type: expected.type, prompt, choices},
    actual, prompt_check: ['tapComplete','gapFill'].includes(expected.type) ? 'Normalized completion-context presence and complete choice multiplicity; exact blank placement not yet proven' : expected.type==='orderTapComplete' ? 'Exact visible scaffold (whitespace ignored), blank runs, example and complete word-bank multiplicity; full answer is not yet visible' : audioPrompt ? 'exact source URL from current-question explicit playback; acoustic output not verified' : exactPrompts.length ? 'normalized equality with independently read visible hint container' : 'normalized presence in current challenge text; exact prompt identity not yet proven'};
};
 const uncovered=function uncoveredReplayType(expectedType, actualType) {
  const supported = ['select','assist','dialogue','gapFill','translate','listenTap','orderTapComplete','match','listenMatch','listenIsolation','listenComplete','listen','completeReverseTranslation','tapComplete','name'];
  return !supported.includes(expectedType) && actualType.split(/\s+/).includes('challenge-' + expectedType);
};
 const currentOnly=true;
 const publicOnly=function publicChallenge(raw, prefix='challenge.0') {
 const allowed=new Set(['options','blankRangeStart','blankRangeEnd','type','prompt','choices','correctIndex','correctIndices','correctSolutions','pairs','tts','slowTts','tokens','displayTokens','sentence','solutionTranslation','newWords','canBeSkipped','image','svg','phrase','hint','learningToken','fromToken','text','value','isBlank','hintTable','range','start','end','character','targetLanguage','sourceLanguage','correctTokens','isSpeaker','hasTts','highlighted','isNewWord','gender','plural','isActive','isAutoGenerated','transliteratedText','hints','hintTokens','example','exampleTokens','canGrade','tapTranslations','word','translation']);
 const omitted=[];const sanitize=(x,path=prefix)=>Array.isArray(x)?x.map((v,i)=>sanitize(v,path+'.'+i)):x&&typeof x==='object'?Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(x).filter(([k])=>{const characterField=/^challenge\.\d+\.character(?:\.|$)/.test(path)&&['url','name','correctAnimation','incorrectAnimation','idleAnimation','avatarIconImage'].includes(k);if(allowed.has(k)||characterField)return true;omitted.push(path+'.'+k);return false}).map(([k,v])=>[k,sanitize(v,path+'.'+k)])):x;

 const content=sanitize(raw);
 return {content,omitted_keys:[...new Set(omitted)]};
};
 const readRendered=(n,sanitizer)=>{
  const publicOnly=(0,eval)('('+sanitizer+')');
  let fiber=n[Object.keys(n).find(k=>k.startsWith('__reactFiber'))];
  for(let depth=0;fiber&&depth<16;depth++,fiber=fiber.return){
   const raw=fiber.memoizedProps?.challenge;
   if(!raw||typeof raw.type!=='string')continue;
   const dom_type=n.getAttribute('data-test');
   if(!dom_type.split(/\s+/).includes('challenge-'+raw.type))throw Error('Rendered/current challenge type mismatch; inspect transition before observing again');
   return {dom_type,visible_text:n.innerText,...publicOnly(raw)};
  }
  throw Error('Current public challenge not found on visible renderer; no fallback to initial session');
 };
 const flowDecision=function currentFlowDecision(facts, labels) {
  if (!['/lesson','/practice'].includes(facts.path) && !/^\/lesson\/(?:unit|section)\/[1-9]\d*\/test$/.test(facts.path)) return {kind:'uncovered_state',reason:'Outside observed exercise route'};
  if (facts.quit_confirmation_count) return {kind:'uncovered_state',reason:'Visible quit confirmation requires an explicit choice'};
  if (facts.dialog_count) return {kind:'uncovered_state',reason:'Visible dialog requires observation'};
  if (facts.path==='/lesson' && facts.challenge_count===0 &&
      facts.body_text.includes('来复习一下这个等级，完成后有惊喜哦！') &&
      facts.review_entry_count===1 && facts.review_entry_enabled)
    return {kind:'review_entry',text:'开始挑战 +20 经验'};
  if (facts.challenge_count===1 && facts.challenge_visible && facts.next_text===labels.check)
    return {kind:'challenge'};
  // Actual stage2 encouragement has exercise progress/quit, session Duo and one bubble.
  // Read its current text instead of enumerating every encouraging phrase.
  if (facts.challenge_count===0 && facts.heading_count===0 && facts.next_text===labels.continue &&
      facts.progress_count===1 && facts.quit_count===1 && facts.duo_count===1 &&
      facts.encouragement_messages?.length===1 && facts.encouragement_messages[0].trim() &&
      facts.continue_count===1 && facts.continue_enabled)
    return {kind:'interlude',text:facts.encouragement_messages[0]};
  const intros=['哎哟不错哦！要不要玩点刺激的？','一起来复习一下之前你不太熟练的部分吧！','万事开头难？可你的表现很不错哦！'];
  const intro=intros.find(text=>facts.body_text.includes(text));
  if (facts.challenge_count===0 && facts.heading_count===0 && intro &&
      facts.continue_count===1 && facts.continue_enabled)
    return {kind:'interlude',text:intro};
  return {kind:'uncovered_state',reason:'Current page is neither a ready challenge nor an observed exercise introduction'};
};
 const keyboardValue=function keyboardTranslationValue(challenge, facts) {
  if (challenge.type==='completeReverseTranslation') {
    if (facts.textbox_count!==1 || !facts.textbox_enabled || facts.bank_count!==0 || facts.textbox_value!=='')
      throw Error('Observed reverse translation requires one empty enabled text field and no word bank');
    const tokens=challenge.displayTokens;
    if (!Array.isArray(tokens) || !tokens.length || !tokens.every(t=>typeof t.text==='string') || !tokens.some(t=>t.isBlank))
      throw Error('Reverse translation has no observed public answer tokens');
    return tokens.map(t=>t.text).join('');
  }
  if (!['translate','name'].includes(challenge.type) || (challenge.choices??[]).length) return null;
  if (facts.textbox_count!==1 || !facts.textbox_enabled || facts.bank_count!==0)
    throw Error('Empty-choice translation requires one actual enabled text field and no word bank');
  const value=challenge.correctSolutions?.[0];
  if (typeof value!=='string' || !value.trim()) throw Error('Current keyboard translation has no public solution');
  return value;
};

 });

 async function exactVisible(locator,text){
   const labels=await locator.allTextContents();
   const indices=labels.flatMap((label,i)=>label.trim().replace(/^\d\s*|\s*\d$/g,'').trim()===text?[i]:[]);
   if(indices.length!==1)throw new Error('Reference option not unique: '+text+' labels='+JSON.stringify(labels));
   await locator.nth(indices[0]).click();
 }

   for(let i=0;i<await matches.count();i++){
    const button=matches.nth(i);
    if(await button.isVisible() && await button.isEnabled()) { await button.click(); return i; }
   }
   throw new Error('No available occurrence of word: '+text);
 }

    }else{
    const bank=page.locator('[data-test="word-bank"]');row.word_bank=await bank.getByRole('button').allTextContents();
    for(const index of c.correctIndices){const choice=c.choices[index],text=typeof choice==='string'?choice:choice.text;
     const occurrence=await clickWord(bank,text);row.actions.push({word:text,occurrence});}
    }
    await page.getByRole('button',{name:labels.check,exact:true}).click();
   }else if(['orderTapComplete','tapComplete'].includes(c.type)){
    const groups=[];let current=null;
    for(const token of c.displayTokens){
     if(token.isBlank){if(!current){current=[];groups.push(current);}current.push(token.text);}
     else if(token.text.trim())current=null;
    }
    row.word_bank=await bank.getByRole('button').allTextContents();
    const gaps=challenge.locator('[aria-label="Selected gap"],[aria-label="Click to select this gap"]');
    if(c.type==='orderTapComplete'&&await gaps.count()!==groups.length)throw Error('Actual gap structure changed before answer');
    for(let index=0;index<groups.length;index++){
     if(c.type==='orderTapComplete'){await gaps.nth(index).click();row.actions.push({gap:index});}
     for(const text of groups[index]){const occurrence=await clickWord(bank,text);row.actions.push({word:text,occurrence,gap:index});}
    }
    row.answer_before_check=await challenge.innerText();
    await page.getByRole('button',{name:labels.check,exact:true}).click();
   }else if(c.type==='listenMatch'){
    const left=challenge.getByRole('button').filter({hasNot:page.locator('[data-test="challenge-tap-token-text"]')});
    const right=challenge.getByRole('button').filter({has:page.locator('[data-test="challenge-tap-token-text"]')});
    if(await left.count()!==c.pairs.length||await right.count()!==c.pairs.length)throw Error('Observed audio columns differ from captured pairs');
    row.right_text=await right.locator('[data-test="challenge-tap-token-text"]').allTextContents();
    row.pair_audio_order=[];row.left_rects=[];row.right_rects=[];
    for(let j=0;j<c.pairs.length;j++){
     row.left_rects.push(await left.nth(j).boundingBox());row.right_rects.push(await right.nth(j).boundingBox());
     const start=await page.evaluate(()=>window.__referenceMedia.length);
     await left.nth(j).click();
     await page.waitForFunction(start=>window.__referenceMedia.slice(start).some(m=>m.src.startsWith('https://d1vq87e9lcf771.cloudfront.net/')),start);
     const urls=await page.evaluate(start=>[...new Set(window.__referenceMedia.slice(start).map(m=>m.src).filter(u=>u.startsWith('https://d1vq87e9lcf771.cloudfront.net/')))],start);
     const matches=c.pairs.filter(p=>urls.includes(p.tts));if(matches.length!==1)throw Error('Actual played audio has no unique pair identity');
     const target=right.filter({hasText:matches[0].translation});if(await target.count()!==1)throw Error('Actual right target not unique');
     await target.click();row.pair_audio_order.push({left_index:j,played_audio:urls,translation:matches[0].translation});
    }
   }else if(c.type==='match'){

    row.button_order=await page.getByRole('button').allTextContents();
    for(const pair of c.pairs){for(const text of [pair.fromToken,pair.learningToken]){await exactVisible(page.getByRole('button'),text);row.actions.push({match:text});}}
   }else throw new Error('Uncovered type: '+c.type);
   await page.getByRole('button',{name:labels.continue,exact:true}).waitFor();
   row.feedback=await page.locator('body').ariaSnapshot();
   row.media=await page.evaluate(start=>window.__referenceMedia.slice(start),mediaStart);
   row.frontend_feedback=await page.evaluate(frontendSurface);
   row.screenshot_feedback="unit12-lesson2-batch02"+'-question-'+record.index+'-feedback.png';
   await page.screenshot({path:row.screenshot_feedback});
   row.rejected=await page.locator('[data-test~="blame-incorrect"]').count()>0;
   if(row.rejected)throw new Error('Source answer rejected');
   if(currentOnly)currentMediaCursor=await page.evaluate(()=>window.__referenceMedia.length);
   await page.getByRole('button',{name:labels.continue,exact:true}).click();
  }catch(error){failure=String(error);break;}
 }
 return {session,observed,interludes,failure,stopped,answered:observed.filter(r=>r.feedback).length,accepted:observed.filter(r=>r.feedback&&r.rejected===false).length,
  step_budget:currentOnly?20:null,steps_completed:observed.filter(r=>r.feedback).length+interludes.length,
  outcome:failure?'failed':stopped?.kind==='uncovered_state'?'uncovered_current_state':stopped?'stopped_at_requested_boundary':currentOnly?(interludes.length&&!observed.length?'current_interlude_replayed':20===1?'current_challenge_replayed':'current_steps_replayed'):'replayed_captured_records',
  final:await page.locator('body').ariaSnapshot(),limitations:['Actual generated exercise observations; public solutions are not blind exploration evidence. Media invocations are not acoustic output evidence. Unknown result/profile pages are preserved without clicking Continue. Current-mode media_before contains only events since this invocation or the previous feedback; unrelated earlier history is not duplicated.']};
})(page);
```
